The scope of this draft is the public text-model and hand-calculation interface; gate clearance is not asserted. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/spec.py:L1-L18]

Current correction to the pinned inventory below (#2532): drilling-stack hand
references now use zero separate contents density, matching the text generator.
The specified drilling-stack mass includes its fluid inventory, even for a
positive bore. Open-water stacks retain separate contents in a continuous bore.
The historical positive-bore exclusions below describe the pinned code revision;
the offline regression covers both conventions, zero bore and zero density.
Hang-off spring tension uses the same fluid-inclusive stack mass convention.
Anti-recoil hold tension also follows that convention: for positive LMRP bore
and contents density, the correction removes the former separate-fluid term
rho_contents * pi * bore_id_m squared * L_LMRP * g / 4 from the hold tension,
matching the generated LMRP line's zero separate contents density.
See the input conventions in `spec.py` and `tests/drilling_riser/global_model/test_stack_contents.py`.

| Revision | Date | What changed and why |

| 4 | 2026-10-19 | Extend the correction to hang-off spring tension and mark superseded inventory statements explicitly. |
| 5 | 2026-10-25 | Record the anti-recoil hold-tension effect and its independent hand balance; clarify section mass conventions and the current positive-bore equilibrium reference. |

## 1. Purpose and scope

The drilling model connects the upper flex-joint pivot, inner barrel, sliding tension ring, riser, lower flex joint and fixed stack datum; the bore contents are separate from structural mass, and buoyancy-equivalent diameter is separate from drag diameter. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/spec.py:L1-L18]
The builder converts the specification to a generic modular-generator input and writes text YAML, with engineering inputs in m, kg, N and Pa converted to model units te, kN, m and kN.m/deg. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/build.py:L1-L5] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/build.py:L112-L143] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/build.py:L475-L492]
Open-water completion models instead connect a tension frame through a laterally restrained, vertically free rotary to the riser and stack; a foundation may replace the fixed datum. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/open_water.py:L3-L20]
Hard/soft hang-off and running configurations are transformations of the drilling specification, with no conductor foundation in the hanging string. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hang_off.py:L3-L16] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hang_off.py:L56-L86]
The parent routing entry has maturity `development`; this is a registry classification, not a claim of validated engineering coverage. [docs/registry/module-routing.yaml:L87-L92]
Geometry closure, positive property ranges and topology constraints are enforced, but no maximum water depth or qualified program-version range is specified by the model schema. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/spec.py:L41-L70] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/spec.py:L366-L436]

UNVERIFIED: the solver-wrapper docstring requests OrcaFlex 11.6; production-version eligibility is not established by that claim; #2530. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/orcaflex_run.py:L1-L6]

## 2. Inputs and outputs

### Input conventions

Global z is positive upward, MSL is zero, and section lists are top to bottom; vessel offset is global (x,y) from the well centre in metres. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/spec.py:L3-L17] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/spec.py:L384-L386]
RAO axes are x forward, y port, z up; rotations are starboard-down roll, bow-down pitch and bow-to-port yaw; phases lead or lag the crest at the RAO origin, and wave directions denote propagation relative to vessel x. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/spec.py:L169-L195]
Current direction is the direction of travel from global x, with depth positive below MSL and terminal speed held beyond the final depth. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/spec.py:L239-L256]
Required means no schema default; unrestricted means no numeric bound is declared for that field, rather than engineering applicability. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/spec.py:L366-L397]

## 3. Method and governing standards

### Text model and hand calculations

| Method / function                                                | Equation or algorithm and basis                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Limits                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `tube_section`                                                   | ID = OD - 2t; A = π(OD²-ID²)/4; I = π(OD⁴-ID⁴)/64; EA = E A; EI = E I [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/spec.py:L30-L38]                                                                                                                                                                                    | positive OD and wall, 2t < OD [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/spec.py:L30-L38]                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `hydrodynamic_od_m`, `winch_tension_n`                           | D = sqrt(4V′/π), with a numerical floor for no displacement; line tension = total vertical target / (count × Δz / sqrt(Δr²+Δz²)) [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/build.py:L44-L61]                                                                                                                        | tensioner geometry uses expected static ring elevation [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/build.py:L51-L61]                                                                                                                                                                |
| `submerged_length_m`, `submerged_weight_n`                       | Lwet = max(0,min(L, -(z_top-L) if z_top>0 else L)); W = g[(m′+ρc π ID²/4)L - ρw V′ Lwet] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L24-L42]                                                                                                                                                          | vertical string, waterline at z=0; g is the implementation constant, not a standards acceptance value [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L24-L42]                                                                                                           |
| **Stack-contents statement superseded by revision 3.** `effective_tension_chain`, `ring_weight_n`, `tension_references` | Tbottom = Ttop - W; Ttop,riser = Tvertical - Wring; ring buoyancy is binary by expected elevation; riser then stack are accumulated downward [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L45-L83]                                                                                                      | conditional mismatch: hand stack weight includes positive bore contents, while drilling stack text generation sets contents density to zero; see #2532 [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/build.py:L208-L212] |
| `tensioned_beam_periods`                                         | EI w'''' - (Te w')' + m w_tt = 0; Hermite bending stiffness Kb, element-average-tension geometric stiffness Kg and consistent mass M are assembled; (Kb+Kg)φ = ω²Mφ; T=2π/ω [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L1-L10] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L98-L146] | prescribed translational pins, optional end rotational springs, nodal masses/springs and bottom clamp; positive eigenvalues required [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L98-L146]                                                                           |
| `beam_model`, `reference_periods`                                | m′dynamic = m′structure + ρc Abore + Ca ρw V′ wet_fraction; subdivided sections interpolate effective tension, ring mass and tensioner geometric spring are added [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L179-L232]                                                                               | upper-to-lower flex-joint span with rigid stack; only `lines` tensioners; small-rotation joint stiffness [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L216-L232]                                                                                                      |
| `tensioner_lateral_stiffness_n_m`                                | kx = Σ (Tline/Lline)(1-ux²), ux = Δr cos(azimuth)/Lline [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L164-L176]                                                                                                                                                                                         | constant-tension inclined lines [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L164-L176]                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `wkb_string_periods`, `wkb_reference_periods`                    | Tn = (2/n) Σ Le/sqrt(Te,average/m′); information-only estimate [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L149-L157] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L235-L238]                                                                                                          | slowly varying positive tension; ring-to-lower-flex-joint span for the specification helper [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L149-L157] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L235-L238]                                           |
| foundation interpolation and springs                             | interpolate p(y) on union grids then linearly in depth; node tributary lengths weight p into force; spring tension = ±p × tributary /1000 kN [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/foundation.py:L19-L64]                                                                                                       | end curves held outside depth range; fixed-base node excluded [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/foundation.py:L19-L52]                                                                                                                                                    |

The matrices and mass regularisation are specified explicitly in the beam implementation, while independent uniform-string and pinned-beam limits are evaluated separately in the tests. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L117-L146] [tests/drilling_riser/global_model/test_hand_checks.py:L66-L79]
Beam periods are returned in order of increasing natural frequency (decreasing periods, fundamental mode first). The selected positive eigenvalues are in ascending order; converting each eigenvalue by `T = 2*pi/sqrt(eigenvalue)` preserves mode identity and gives decreasing periods. The docstring and closed-form tests describe this same order. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L105-L107] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L142-L146] [tests/drilling_riser/global_model/test_hand_checks.py:test_tensioned_string_limit_matches_closed_form]

**Worked example: uniform-string limit.** The synthetic inputs are length `L = 300 m`, tension `Te = 2,000,000 N` and mass per length `m = 800 kg/m`, matching `test_tensioned_string_limit_matches_closed_form`. The method is `v = sqrt(Te/m)` and `f_n = n*v/(2*L)`, from [OpenStax, University Physics Volume 1 (2016), section 16.6, equation 16.16](https://openstax.org/books/university-physics-volume-1/pages/16-6-standing-waves-and-resonance). Independent substitution gives `v = 50 m/s` and `T_n = 1/f_n = 12/n s`: modes one through five have periods `12.0, 6.0, 4.0, 3.0, 2.4 s`. The finite-element fixture uses the same small positive bending stiffness and mesh as the existing string-limit test; displayed answers are rounded to one decimal place, while that test retains its numerical error criterion.

```python
>>> from digitalmodel.drilling_riser.global_model.hand_checks import BeamSegment, tensioned_beam_periods
>>> # Synthetic inputs and mesh: test_hand_checks.py::test_tensioned_string_limit_matches_closed_form.
>>> length, tension, mass = 300.0, 2.0e6, 800.0
>>> segments = [BeamSegment(length / 300, 1.0, tension, tension, mass) for _ in range(300)]
>>> periods = tensioned_beam_periods(segments, n_modes=5)
>>> # Expected values: independent T_n = 12/n s calculation above; rounding is for display only.
>>> [round(period, 1) for period in periods]
[12.0, 6.0, 4.0, 3.0, 2.4]

```

The example is executed by `test_natural_period_ordering_worked_example`; it illustrates return order for synthetic inputs and does not establish qualification of a riser design. [tests/drilling_riser/global_model/test_period_ordering_docs.py:test_natural_period_ordering_worked_example]

Superseded by revision 3: the pinned drilling stack hand-reference mismatch is conditional on positive bore area and contents density: ΔW = ρc Abore L g N per stack section; zero-bore fixtures do not expose it, while open-water generation and hand references both carry separate contents. The proposed first-step riser estimate will integrate the small-slope, tension-dominated limit of the beam equation: H_hand = delta_ring / sum(L_i / Tmean_i) (N), using horizontal ring-to-lower-pivot displacement in m, riser-chain section lengths in m and positive mean effective tensions in N. It will compare native global horizontal reaction with H_hand in the same direction and within 20.0% in magnitude, under AQG r0 §6; this estimate excludes distributed lateral loads, significant bending and the inner barrel/tensioner subsystem. The equation is a hand reduction of the implemented beam equation, not a returned module output. ## 9. Dependencies

### Upstream and interface boundaries

The parent `calc_contracts` loader consumes external mappings keyed by dataset and calculation, rejects duplicate keys and malformed contract blocks, and returns source-page provenance; the external values are not embedded in this module. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/calc_contracts.py:L93-L125] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/calc_contracts.py:L152-L158]
Parent `stackup` functions use kN for top/effective tension, mm and MPa for thickness, and weight densities in kN/m³ for slip-ring tension; these inputs are not interchangeable with the global-model SI schema without conversion. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/stackup.py:L28-L50] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/stackup.py:L53-L108] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/stackup.py:L111-L145]
The downstream `campaign` adapter consumes specifications, references, model generation and extraction; `postprocess.stress_range.significant_range` calls `fatigue_channels.half_cycles` when explicit half-cycle ranges are absent. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/campaign.py:L63-L64] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/campaign.py:L848-L878] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/campaign.py:L912-L987] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/postprocess/stress_range.py:L145-L154]
Text generation depends on the modular generator and its ProjectInputSpec; the solver-facing routines resolve the binding only through `_api()` when called. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/build.py:L475-L492] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/orcaflex_run.py:L15-L39]
UNVERIFIED: external campaign consumer inventory, dataset revisions/digests and production solver-version eligibility cannot be established from public interfaces alone; #2531.

Every returned drilling, open-water and hang-off w5 payload rounds finite float fields to 6 significant digits through `compact`; integer counts, strings, booleans and None remain unchanged. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/w5_channels.py:L29-L30] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/w5_channels.py:L58-L74] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/w5_channels.py:L366-L366] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/w5_channels.py:L455-L455] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/w5_channels.py:L591-L591]
The adapter also applies `w5_channels.compact` with that precision to both optional fatigue payloads before attaching them to w5. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/campaign.py:L948-L950] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/campaign.py:L973-L975]

Fatigue stresses/ranges use kPa and half cycles carry weight 0.5 in the damage sum; this extractor returns histograms and invariant flags, not fatigue damage or life. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/fatigue_channels.py:L104-L137]
`as_analysed_sections` additionally reads line/section identity, arc/length/elevations/segment size, diameters, dry/contents/wet/added mass, EA/EI/GJ, coefficients and drag factor; it is not called by the public adapter extraction and does not cover non-line components. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/orcaflex_run.py:L248-L289] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/campaign.py:L912-L987]

### Related implementations across the repository

The method search covers repository Python sources, including `scripts/`, tests and examples; the following related implementations have distinct assumptions and are not promoted to independent references by their presence.

| Implementation | Relationship and limitation |
| --- | --- |
| `infrastructure.base_solvers.structural.elements.beam_element.BeamElement` | related two-node Euler-Bernoulli element with displacement/rotation degrees of freedom; no independent qualification is assigned here [src/digitalmodel/infrastructure/base_solvers/structural/elements/beam_element.py:L13-L25] |
| `subsea.viv_analysis.frequency_calculator.FrequencyCalculator.calculate_natural_frequency` | related beam/tension-controlled member frequency interface; no independent qualification of the assembled riser model is assigned here [src/digitalmodel/subsea/viv_analysis/frequency_calculator.py:L59-L92] |
| `scripts/orcaflex/prepare_slay_campaign.py::catenary`, `submerged_weight` | inextensible touchdown catenary and empty coated-pipe weight (kN/m), distinct from this module's vertical effective-tension chain and separate contents [scripts/orcaflex/prepare_slay_campaign.py:L24-L63] |
| `scripts/python/digitalmodel/analysis/catenary_solver.py` | shape/slope/arc-length objective, not a riser beam or qualified reference [scripts/python/digitalmodel/analysis/catenary_solver.py:L8-L50] |
| `marine_ops.marine_analysis.catenary.solver.CatenarySolver` | general endpoint catenary with EA input; the conflicting reference fixture in #2050 is excluded as a hand-check reference [src/digitalmodel/marine_ops/marine_analysis/catenary/solver.py:L30-L39] [src/digitalmodel/marine_ops/marine_analysis/catenary/solver.py:L77-L118] |

#### Effective-tension method boundaries

`FrequencyCalculator.calculate_effective_tension` computes top tension minus steel-minus-displaced-water weight per metre times depth, in N, with no contents term; it is independent of `tension_references` and is not compared on the section-4 chain case. Its contents convention cannot substitute for the separate-contents chain without reconciling the inputs; the pinned stack-contents discrepancy under #2532 is superseded by revision 3. [src/digitalmodel/subsea/viv_analysis/frequency_calculator.py:L248-L286] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L37-L55] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L64-L83]
`TypicalRiserStackUpCalculations.evaluate_effective_tension` seeds a component from overpull with an inline force conversion and marches component service weights below and above it; this legacy chain is independent of `tension_references` and is not compared on the section-4 chain case. [src/digitalmodel/infrastructure/base_solvers/marine/typical_riser_stack_up_calculations.py:L415-L490]
Parent `stackup.effective_tension` returns `top_tension_kn - submerged_weight_kn * depth_factor`, in kN; it is an independent scalar reducer, not a wrapper or caller of `tension_references`. The latter forms sectionwise N-valued chains using wetted lengths and separate contents, so supplying a reconciled total weight and converting units is a prerequisite for comparison; no numerical comparison is recorded here. [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/stackup.py:L85-L108] [src/digitalmodel/drilling_riser/global_model/hand_checks.py:L37-L83]

Pinned-tree inventory record (2026-10-15): 1,656 map entries are retained, one row per implementation within each of the six families below; five entries across three paths are absent at this pack's code pin and have no invented pinned citation. The absent paths are `tests/solvers/orcaflex/test_slay_layout.py`, `tests/solvers/orcaflex/test_slay_load_form.py` and `src/digitalmodel/subsea/on_bottom_stability/e305_simplified.py`.
Implementation paths and line ranges appear in the row citations; “this pack” denotes the header path. Rows describe pinned software operations, including dispatch, examples and test helpers; map membership does not establish that a helper independently implements the family physics. A copy marker is distinguished from verified identical source bytes or bodies. “Not checked” records that agreement with the pack's key controls has not been demonstrated, and “none” means no covering pack is assigned by this inventory.
The two reviewed effective-tension implementations and the parent scalar reducer are present in `effective-tension-riser-stack-up`; none of those three is a map miss. The retained catenary references above remain outside qualification under #2050.
